# Row compositions (v3)

Every numeric cell uses **Willow Cove (i5-1135G7) / Golden Cove (i7-12700K)** order; unavailable measurements are `-`. Shared context: [BENCHMARKS.md](../../BENCHMARKS.md).

Each factor is base latency divided by candidate latency for the same public call, field, coefficient pattern, and geometry. **Above one, the candidate is faster.** Base and candidate are separately built artifacts of identical benchmark sources.

## Find a result

- [Setup](#setup)
- [Outcomes](#outcomes)
- [Scatter](#scatter)
- [Gather](#gather)
- [Contiguous matrix](#contiguous-matrix)
- [Offset matrix](#offset-matrix)
- [Single row](#single-row)
- [Row addition](#row-addition)
- [Routing boundaries](#routing-boundaries)
- [Field definitions](#field-definitions)
- [Measurement details](#measurement-details)

## Setup

| Setting | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Base revision | `e9db2f121a3ebdef20de43abd3c1b1f2a0342035` |
| Candidate revision | `f43587f547023f769c5e05794f21b5054ecd6869` |
| Toolchain | 1.98.1 |
| Geometry | 4 destination rows, 3 sources |
| Main tables | all features; `v4x` / `v3_gfni_crypto` requests |
| Protocol | Five interleaved base/candidate pairs per host, feature set, and request; shuffled pair order. |
| Aggregation | Median of five per-run medians; factors from unrounded aggregates. |

## Outcomes

Cell counts across every field, operation, pattern, and geometry. A cell is unresolved when its field geometry is resolution-limited or its duplicate controls moved between artifacts, or when fewer than four of five paired rounds agree in direction.

## Measurement details

<details>
<summary>Hosts, controls, and classification</summary>

- Hosts: Willow Cove i5-1135G7 pinned to core 3; Golden Cove i7-12700K pinned to core 8.
- Both hosts ran with intel_pstate turbo disabled, the performance governor, and normal (SCHED_OTHER) scheduling for every pilot and paired run.
- Portable release benchmark profile with empty RUSTFLAGS; identical benchmark sources, compiler, and lock in both artifacts. Main tables use the all-features build; the outcome table also covers the default build.
- Every timed body black-boxes its inputs and the complete output buffer. Fixtures, prepared coefficients, and allocations stay outside the timed region; unprepared calls include their per-call coefficient preparation.
- Each operation is checked once against an independent element oracle before timing, in both artifacts.
- Classification: each field and geometry carries a band of twice the larger of its duplicate elementwise-control gap in a five-run base-only pilot and the base-to-candidate movement of those unchanged controls in the boundary campaign. A cell is a tie inside its band; a win or slower only outside it with at least four of five paired rounds agreeing; otherwise unresolved.
- Fill, copy, and no-op shapes (zero and unit coefficients) run for a few nanoseconds per call; their factors move with buffer placement and code layout between separately built artifacts.
- Willow Cove resolves the v4x request with simd512; the default build resolves the same request to v3_gfni_crypto.

</details>
